/* Outputs a byte to the specified hardware port */
static inline void outportb( uint32_t port, uint8_t value ) {
     d0f:	55                   	push   %rbp
     d10:	48 89 e5             	mov    %rsp,%rbp
     d13:	89 7d fc             	mov    %edi,-0x4(%rbp)
     d16:	89 f0                	mov    %esi,%eax
     d18:	88 45 f8             	mov    %al,-0x8(%rbp)
    __asm__ __volatile__ ("outb %%al,%%dx"::"d" (port), "a" (value));

     d1e:	0f b6 45 f8          	movzbl -0x8(%rbp),%eax
     d22:	ee                   	out    %al,(%dx)
